```yaml
```yaml
lines:
  - id: k1p.h_zero
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Zero"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: sales.zero}

  - id: k1p.h_mp_t1
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Test 1: you participated in the activity for more than 500 hours during the tax year"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: [k1p.work, {const: true}]}, {op: test, eq: [k1p.hours500, {const: true}]}]}

  - id: k1p.h_mp_gp
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Tests 2, 3, 4 and 7, available only to a general partner (a limited partner must meet item 1, 5, or 6)"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [k1p.general, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [k1p.work, {const: true}]}
        - op: test
          any:
            - {op: test, eq: [k1p.nearly_all, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [k1p.most_hours, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [k1p.t4, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [k1p.t7, {const: true}]}

  - id: k1p.h_material
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "You materially participated in the activity (one or more of the material participation tests apply)"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - {op: test, eq: [k1p.h_mp_t1, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [k1p.five_of_ten, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [k1p.t6, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [k1p.h_mp_gp, {const: true}]}

  - id: k1p.h_has_prior1
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Prior-year unallowed loss from the box 1 activity"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: [k1p.prior_any, {const: true}]}, {op: test, eq: [k1p.prior_b1_any, {const: true}]}]}

  - id: k1p.h_prior1
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Prior-year unallowed loss, box 1 activity (2024 Form 8582, Part VII, column (c))"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [k1p.h_has_prior1, {const: true}]}, then: k1p.prior_b1}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [k1p.h_has_prior1, {const: false}]}, then: k1p.h_zero}

  - id: k1p.h_has_prior2
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Prior-year unallowed loss from the box 2 activity"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: [k1p.prior_any, {const: true}]}, {op: test, eq: [k1p.prior_b2_any, {const: true}]}]}

  - id: k1p.h_prior2
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Prior-year unallowed loss, box 2 activity (2024 Form 8582, Part VII, column (c))"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [k1p.h_has_prior2, {const: true}]}, then: k1p.prior_b2}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [k1p.h_has_prior2, {const: false}]}, then: k1p.h_zero}

  - id: k1p.h_has_prior3
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Prior-year unallowed loss from the box 3 activity"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: [k1p.prior_any, {const: true}]}, {op: test, eq: [k1p.prior_b3_any, {const: true}]}]}

  - id: k1p.h_prior3
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Prior-year unallowed loss, box 3 activity (2024 Form 8582, Part VII, column (c))"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [k1p.h_has_prior3, {const: true}]}, then: k1p.prior_b3}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [k1p.h_has_prior3, {const: false}]}, then: k1p.h_zero}

  - id: k1p.h_b1_posraw
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Box 1 income (positive part)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: subtract_floor0, from: k1p.box1, take: {const: 0}}

  - id: k1p.h_b1_negraw
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Box 1 loss (as a positive amount)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: subtract_floor0, from: k1p.h_zero, take: k1p.box1}

  - id: k1p.h_b1_pos
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Current year net income, box 1 activity"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [k1p.has_b1, {const: true}]}, then: k1p.h_b1_posraw}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [k1p.has_b1, {const: false}]}, then: k1p.h_zero}

  - id: k1p.h_b1_neg
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Current year net loss, box 1 activity"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [k1p.has_b1, {const: true}]}, then: k1p.h_b1_negraw}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [k1p.has_b1, {const: false}]}, then: k1p.h_zero}

  - id: k1p.h_b1_none
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "No box 1 amount and no prior-year unallowed box 1 loss"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: [k1p.has_b1, {const: false}]}, {op: test, eq: [k1p.h_has_prior1, {const: false}]}]}

  - id: k1p.h_b1_np
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Box 1 activity is nonpassive: you materially participated, or the activity is trading personal property for the account of owners"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, any: [{op: test, eq: [k1p.h_material, {const: true}]}, {op: test, eq: [k1p.trader, {const: true}]}]}

  - id: k1p.h_b1_pass
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Box 1 trade or business activity in which you didn't materially participate (passive)"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: [k1p.h_b1_none, {const: false}]}, {op: test, eq: [k1p.h_b1_np, {const: false}]}]}

  - id: k1p.h_b1_former
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Box 1 activity is nonpassive this year (a former passive activity if it has a prior-year unallowed loss)"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: [k1p.h_b1_none, {const: false}]}, {op: test, eq: [k1p.h_b1_np, {const: true}]}]}

  - id: k1p.h_b1_min
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Former passive activity: current year net income up to the amount of prior-year unallowed loss"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: smaller_of, of: [k1p.h_b1_pos, k1p.h_prior1]}

  - id: k1p.h_b1_in_v
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Box 1 activity is entered in Part V"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - {op: test, eq: [k1p.h_b1_pass, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: [k1p.h_b1_former, {const: true}]}, {op: test, eq: [k1p.h_has_prior1, {const: true}]}]}

  - id: k1p.h_b2_posraw
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Box 2 income (positive part)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: subtract_floor0, from: k1p.box2, take: {const: 0}}

  - id: k1p.h_b2_negraw
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Box 2 loss (as a positive amount)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: subtract_floor0, from: k1p.h_zero, take: k1p.box2}

  - id: k1p.h_b2_pos
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Current year net income, box 2 activity"

      alternatives:

        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [k1p.has_b2, {const: false}]}, then: k1p.h_zero}

        - {op: test, gt: [k1p.h_b1_neg, {const: 0}]}
        - {op: test, gt: [k1p.h_b2_neg, {const: 0}]}
        - {op: test, gt: [k1p.h_b3_neg, {const: 0}]}

  - id: k1p.h_loss_limited
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Losses limited by the basis or at-risk limitations (applied before the passive activity limitations)"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [k1p.h_any_loss, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, any: [{op: test, eq: [k1p.basis_short, {const: true}]}, {op: test, eq: [k1p.not_at_risk, {const: true}]}]}
    note: "The basis and at-risk limitations apply before the passive activity rules and are not computed here; a loss subject to either is refused on the loss lines."

  - id: k1p.screened
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "This Schedule K-1 has an item outside the supported scope"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - {op: test, eq: [k1p.ptp, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [k1p.r_b4b, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [k1p.r_b7, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [k1p.r_b10, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [k1p.r_b11, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [k1p.r_b12, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [k1p.r_b13, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [k1p.r_b14bc, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [k1p.r_b15, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [k1p.r_b16, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [k1p.r_b17, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [k1p.r_b20, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [k1p.r_b21, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [k1p.r_b2223, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [k1p.r_foreign, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [k1p.r_disposed, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [k1p.upe, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [k1p.interest_buy, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [k1p.basis_carry, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [k1p.risk_carry, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [k1p.fuel_2024, {const: true}]}
    note: "The pages do not define 'screened'. Read as: any of this K-1's screening inputs is answered yes. PTP rules, unreimbursed partnership expenses, debt-financed acquisition interest, prior basis/at-risk losses (PYA) and the 2024 fuel credit add-back to column (h)/(k) are not computed. Inputs asked only conditionally (dist_over, b19_other, z_other) are left out."

  - id: k1p.b1_net_income
    printed: "V (a) (k1p.b1_net_income)"
    label: "Part V, column (a): current year net income from the box 1 activity (for a former passive activity, the current year net income up to the amount of prior-year unallowed loss)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [k1p.h_b1_pass, {const: true}]}, then: k1p.h_b1_pos}
        - guard: {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: [k1p.h_b1_pass, {const: false}]}, {op: test, eq: [k1p.h_b1_former, {const: true}]}]}
          then: k1p.h_b1_min
        - guard: {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: [k1p.h_b1_pass, {const: false}]}, {op: test, eq: [k1p.h_b1_former, {const: false}]}]}
          then: k1p.h_zero
    shown_when: k1p.h_b1_in_v
    note: "A former passive activity (nonpassive this year with a prior-year unallowed loss) enters current year net income only up to the prior-year unallowed loss (i8582 Former Passive Activities); the excess goes to column (k)."

  - id: k1p.b1_net_loss
    printed: "V (b) (k1p.b1_net_loss)"
    label: "Part V, column (b): current year net loss from the box 1 activity"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [k1p.h_b1_pass, {const: true}]}, then: k1p.h_b1_neg}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [k1p.h_b1_pass, {const: false}]}, then: k1p.h_zero}
    refuses: [k1p.h_loss_limited]
    shown_when: k1p.h_b1_in_v
    note: "For a former passive activity with a current year net loss, only the prior-year unallowed loss is entered (column (c)); the current loss is nonpassive (column (i))."

  - id: k1p.h_has_rent
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "The partnership has a rental activity (box 2 or 3, or prior-year unallowed loss from one)"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - {op: test, eq: [k1p.has_b2, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [k1p.has_b3, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [k1p.h_has_prior2, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [k1p.h_has_prior3, {const: true}]}

  - id: k1p.h_dwell
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "The rental of a dwelling unit any partner used for personal purposes for more than the greater of 14 days or 10% of the days rented at fair rental value (nonpassive)"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: [k1p.h_has_rent, {const: true}]}, {op: test, eq: [k1p.dwelling, {const: true}]}]}
    note: "The dwelling-unit rule is applied to both rental boxes (2 and 3); the text names it for rental activities generally."

  - id: k1p.h_b2_none
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "No box 2 amount and no prior-year unallowed box 2 loss"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: [k1p.has_b2, {const: false}]}, {op: test, eq: [k1p.h_has_prior2, {const: false}]}]}

  - id: k1p.h_b2_np
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Box 2 is not from a passive activity: you were a real estate professional and materially participated, or the dwelling-unit rule applies"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - op: test
          all:
            - {op: test, eq: [k1p.h_b2_none, {const: false}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [k1p.re_pro, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [k1p.h_material, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [k1p.h_dwell, {const: true}]}

  - id: k1p.h_b2_former
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Box 2 activity is nonpassive this year"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: [k1p.h_b2_none, {const: false}]}, {op: test, eq: [k1p.h_b2_np, {const: true}]}]}

  - id: k1p.h_b2_active
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Passive rental real estate with active participation (Part IV): general partner, helped make management decisions, owned at least 10%, and not married filing separately having lived with spouse"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [k1p.h_b2_none, {const: false}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [k1p.h_b2_np, {const: false}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [k1p.general, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [k1p.decisions, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [k1p.tenth, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [f8582.mfs_together, {const: false}]}
    note: "Limited partners can't actively participate; married individuals filing separately who lived with their spouses at any time must use Part V. The latter is read from f8582.mfs_together."

  - id: k1p.h_b2_pv
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Passive box 2 activity that doesn't qualify for the special allowance (Part V)"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [k1p.h_b2_none, {const: false}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [k1p.h_b2_np, {const: false}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [k1p.h_b2_active, {const: false}]}

  - id: k1p.h_b2_in_v
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Box 2 current year amounts are entered in Part V"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - {op: test, eq: [k1p.h_b2_pv, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: [k1p.h_b2_former, {const: true}]}, {op: test, eq: [k1p.h_has_prior2, {const: true}]}]}

  - id: k1p.h_b2_prior_iv
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Prior-year unallowed box 2 loss goes in Part IV, column (c) (active participation in both the year the loss arose and the current year)"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [k1p.h_b2_active, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, any: [{op: test, eq: [k1p.h_has_prior2, {const: false}]}, {op: test, eq: [k1p.prior_not_active, {const: false}]}]}

  - id: k1p.h_b2_prior_v
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Prior-year unallowed box 2 loss goes in Part V, column (c)"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: [k1p.h_has_prior2, {const: true}]}, {op: test, eq: [k1p.h_b2_prior_iv, {const: false}]}]}

  - id: k1p.p4_income
    printed: "IV (a)"
    label: "Part IV, column (a): current year net income (rental real estate activities with active participation)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [k1p.h_b2_active, {const: true}]}, then: k1p.b2_net_income}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [k1p.h_b2_active, {const: false}]}, then: k1p.h_zero}
    refuses: [k1p.screened]
    shown_when: k1p.h_b2_active

  - id: k1p.p4_loss
    printed: "IV (b)"
    label: "Part IV, column (b): current year net loss"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [k1p.h_b2_active, {const: true}]}, then: k1p.b2_net_loss}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [k1p.h_b2_active, {const: false}]}, then: k1p.h_zero}
    refuses: [k1p.screened, k1p.h_loss_limited]
    shown_when: k1p.h_b2_active

  - id: k1p.p4_prior
    printed: "IV (c)"
    label: "Part IV, column (c): prior-year unallowed losses (2024 Form 8582, Part VII, column (c)); only if you actively participated in both the year the loss arose and the current tax year"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [k1p.h_b2_prior_iv, {const: true}]}, then: k1p.h_prior2}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [k1p.h_b2_prior_iv, {const: false}]}, then: k1p.h_zero}
    refuses: [k1p.screened]
    shown_when: k1p.h_b2_active

  - id: k1p.h_p4_neg
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Part IV columns (b) and (c) combined"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [k1p.p4_loss, k1p.p4_prior]}

  - id: k1p.p6_loss
    printed: "VI (a)"
    label: "Part VI, column (a): the overall loss from column (e) of Part IV for the activity"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: subtract_floor0, from: k1p.h_p4_neg, take: k1p.p4_income}
    refuses: [k1p.screened]
    shown_when: k1p.h_p6_on
    note: "Figured from this K-1's own Part IV entries (columns (a)-(c) combined) rather than k1p.b2_overall_loss, because the prior-year loss may go to Part V while the current year amounts are in Part IV."

  - id: k1p.h_p6_on
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Part VI completed (an amount other than zero on Part II, line 9) and the activity has an overall loss in Part IV, column (e)"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, gt: [f8582.line9, {const: 0}]}
        - {op: test, gt: [k1p.p6_loss, {const: 0}]}

  - id: k1p.h_b2_v_income
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Part V, column (a), box 2 activity"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [k1p.h_b2_in_v, {const: true}]}, then: k1p.b2_net_income}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [k1p.h_b2_in_v, {const: false}]}, then: k1p.h_zero}

  - id: k1p.h_b2_v_loss
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Part V, column (b), box 2 activity"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [k1p.h_b2_pv, {const: true}]}, then: k1p.b2_net_loss}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [k1p.h_b2_pv, {const: false}]}, then: k1p.h_zero}

  - id: k1p.h_b2_v_prior
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Part V, column (c), box 2 activity"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [k1p.h_b2_prior_iv, {const: false}]}, then: k1p.h_prior2}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [k1p.h_b2_prior_iv, {const: true}]}, then: k1p.h_zero}

  - id: k1p.h_b3_none
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "No box 3 amount and no prior-year unallowed box 3 loss"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: [k1p.has_b3, {const: false}]}, {op: test, eq: [k1p.h_has_prior3, {const: false}]}]}

  - id: k1p.h_b3_former
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Box 3 activity is nonpassive this year (dwelling-unit rule)"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: [k1p.h_b3_none, {const: false}]}, {op: test, eq: [k1p.h_dwell, {const: true}]}]}

  - id: k1p.h_b3_pv
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Box 3 is a passive activity amount"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: [k1p.h_b3_none, {const: false}]}, {op: test, eq: [k1p.h_dwell, {const: false}]}]}

  - id: k1p.h_b3_in_v
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Box 3 current year amounts are entered in Part V"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - {op: test, eq: [k1p.h_b3_pv, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: [k1p.h_b3_former, {const: true}]}, {op: test, eq: [k1p.h_has_prior3, {const: true}]}]}

  - id: k1p.h_b3_v_income
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Part V, column (a), box 3 activity"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [k1p.h_b3_in_v, {const: true}]}, then: k1p.b3_net_income}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [k1p.h_b3_in_v, {const: false}]}, then: k1p.h_zero}

  - id: k1p.h_b3_v_loss
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Part V, column (b), box 3 activity"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [k1p.h_b3_pv, {const: true}]}, then: k1p.b3_net_loss}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [k1p.h_b3_pv, {const: false}]}, then: k1p.h_zero}

  - id: k1p.h_in_v_any
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "An activity of this partnership is entered in Part V"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - {op: test, eq: [k1p.h_b1_in_v, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [k1p.h_b2_in_v, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [k1p.h_b2_prior_v, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [k1p.h_b3_in_v, {const: true}]}

  - id: k1p.p5_income
    printed: "V (a) (k1p.p5_income)"
    label: "Part V, column (a): current year net income (passive activities other than rental real estate with active participation)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [k1p.b1_net_income, k1p.h_b2_v_income, k1p.h_b3_v_income]}
    refuses: [k1p.screened]
    shown_when: k1p.h_in_v_any

  - id: k1p.p5_loss
    printed: "V (b) (k1p.p5_loss)"
    label: "Part V, column (b): current year net loss"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [k1p.b1_net_loss, k1p.h_b2_v_loss, k1p.h_b3_v_loss]}
    refuses: [k1p.screened, k1p.h_loss_limited]
    shown_when: k1p.h_in_v_any

  - id: k1p.p5_prior
    printed: "V (c)"
    label: "Part V, column (c): unallowed losses for the prior years (2024 Form 8582, Part VII, column (c))"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [k1p.h_prior1, k1p.h_b2_v_prior, k1p.h_prior3]}
    refuses: [k1p.screened]
    shown_when: k1p.h_in_v_any
    note: "Includes prior-year unallowed losses of former passive activities (i8582) and of box 2 losses from a year without active participation (Part IV, column (c) instructions)."

  - id: k1p.h_b1_excess
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Box 1 nonpassive net income in excess of the prior-year unallowed loss"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: subtract_floor0, from: k1p.h_b1_pos, take: k1p.h_b1_min}

  - id: k1p.h_b2_min
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Box 2 former passive activity: net income up to the prior-year unallowed loss"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: smaller_of, of: [k1p.h_b2_pos, k1p.h_prior2]}

  - id: k1p.h_b2_excess
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Box 2 nonpassive net income in excess of the prior-year unallowed loss"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: subtract_floor0, from: k1p.h_b2_pos, take: k1p.h_b2_min}

  - id: k1p.h_b3_min
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Box 3 former passive activity: net income up to the prior-year unallowed loss"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: smaller_of, of: [k1p.h_b3_pos, k1p.h_prior3]}

  - id: k1p.h_b3_excess
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Box 3 nonpassive net income in excess of the prior-year unallowed loss"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: subtract_floor0, from: k1p.h_b3_pos, take: k1p.h_b3_min}

  - id: k1p.h_b1_k
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Box 1 nonpassive income, column (k)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [k1p.h_b1_former, {const: true}]}, then: k1p.h_b1_excess}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [k1p.h_b1_former, {const: false}]}, then: k1p.h_zero}

  - id: k1p.h_b2_k
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Box 2 nonpassive income, column (k)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [k1p.h_b2_former, {const: true}]}, then: k1p.h_b2_excess}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [k1p.h_b2_former, {const: false}]}, then: k1p.h_zero}

  - id: k1p.h_b3_k
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Box 3 nonpassive income, column (k)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [k1p.h_b3_former, {const: true}]}, then: k1p.h_b3_excess}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [k1p.h_b3_former, {const: false}]}, then: k1p.h_zero}

  - id: k1p.h_gp
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Box 4a guaranteed payments for services (not passive income; column (k))"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [k1p.has_b4a, {const: true}]}, then: k1p.box4a}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [k1p.has_b4a, {const: false}]}, then: k1p.h_zero}

  - id: k1p.h_k_a
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Column (k), boxes 1 and 2"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [k1p.h_b1_k, k1p.h_b2_k]}

  - id: k1p.h_k_b
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Column (k), box 3 and box 4a"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [k1p.h_b3_k, k1p.h_gp]}

  - id: k1p.col_k
    printed: "28 (k)"
    label: "Schedule E, line 28, column (k): nonpassive income from Schedule K-1"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [k1p.h_k_a, k1p.h_k_b]}
    refuses: [k1p.screened]
    note: "Nonpassive income from boxes 1-3 (material participation, real estate professional, trading or dwelling-unit rules) beyond any prior-year unallowed loss of a former passive activity, plus box 4a guaranteed payments."

  - id: k1p.h_b1_i
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Box 1 nonpassive loss, column (i)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [k1p.h_b1_former, {const: true}]}, then: k1p.h_b1_neg}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [k1p.h_b1_former, {const: false}]}, then: k1p.h_zero}

  - id: k1p.h_b2_i
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Box 2 nonpassive loss, column (i)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [k1p.h_b2_former, {const: true}]}, then: k1p.h_b2_neg}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [k1p.h_b2_former, {const: false}]}, then: k1p.h_zero}

  - id: k1p.h_b3_i
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Box 3 nonpassive loss, column (i)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [k1p.h_b3_former, {const: true}]}, then: k1p.h_b3_neg}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [k1p.h_b3_former, {const: false}]}, then: k1p.h_zero}

  - id: k1p.h_i_a
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Column (i), boxes 1 and 2"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [k1p.h_b1_i, k1p.h_b2_i]}

  - id: k1p.col_i
    printed: "28 (i)"
    label: "Schedule E, line 28, column (i): nonpassive loss from Schedule K-1"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [k1p.h_i_a, k1p.h_b3_i]}
    refuses: [k1p.screened, k1p.h_loss_limited]
    note: "Current year losses of nonpassive activities, after basis and at-risk limitations (refused when either limits the loss). Prior-year basis/at-risk losses (PYA) and UPE are screened out."
